Onstart:
// ================================
// 1. INITIAL STATE
// ================================
Set(varAppLoaded, false);

Set(paramPlannerID, 0);
Set(paramPlannerRowID, 0);
Set(varEvalRecordReady, false);
Set(varPlannerMode, "New");

Set(gblActiveEvaluationID, Blank());
Set(gblPlannerID, Blank());
Set(gblPlannerRowID, Blank());

Set(varResolvedEvalItemID, Blank());
Set(varEvalRec, Blank());
Set(gblEvalItemID, Blank());
Set(varEditLockMinutes, 20);

Set(varChecklistPreloaded, false);
Set(varLoadedHazardsEvalID, Blank());
Set(varLoadedCOSHHEvalID, Blank());

// ================================
// 2. CURRENT USER IDENTITY / ALIASES
// ================================
Set(varUserEmail, Lower(Trim(User().Email)));
Set(_my, IfError(Office365Users.MyProfileV2(), Blank()));

// Build normalized aliases
Set(varUserUPN, Lower(Coalesce(_my.userPrincipalName, User().Email, "")));
Set(varUserSMTP, Lower(Coalesce(_my.mail, "")));

// Build alias list as [{Email:"..."}]
Clear(colUserEmails);
Collect(colUserEmails, { Email: varUserEmail });

If(!IsBlank(varUserUPN),  Collect(colUserEmails, { Email: varUserUPN }));
If(!IsBlank(varUserSMTP), Collect(colUserEmails, { Email: varUserSMTP }));

// Remove blanks
RemoveIf(colUserEmails, IsBlank(Email));

// De-duplicate
ClearCollect(
    colUserEmails,
    ForAll(
        GroupBy(colUserEmails, 'Email', 'grp'),
        { Email: Email }
    )
);


// ================================
// 3. GLOBAL ROLE FLAGS
// ================================

// App admin
// ================================
// 3. GLOBAL ROLE FLAGS
// ================================

// App admin
ClearCollect(
    colAppAdmins,
    AddColumns(
        Filter('RAMS App Roles', IsActive = true && Role.Value = "Admin"),
        'UserEmail', 
        Lower(Coalesce(ThisRecord.'User'.Email, Substitute(ThisRecord.'User'.Claims, "i:0#.f|membership|", ""), ""))
    )
);
Set(
    varIsAppAdmin,
    !IsBlank(
        LookUp(
            colAppAdmins,
            CountIf(colUserEmails As ue, ue.Email = UserEmail) > 0
        )
    )
);

// Optional reusable Project -> H&S map
ClearCollect(
    colProjectHSMap,
    AddColumns(
        Filter('RAMS Project H&S Mapping', IsActive = true),
        'ProjectName', Title,
        'UserEmail', Lower(Coalesce(HSValidator.Email, "")),
        'HSValidatorRecord', HSValidator // This saves the full Person record directly from SharePoint
    )
);


// Global H&S flag (across known project map)
Set(
    varIsHS,
    CountIf(
        colProjectHSMap,
        CountIf(colUserEmails, Email = Lower(UserEmail)) > 0
    ) > 0
);


// ================================
// 4. REFRESH CONNECTIONS
// ================================
If(
    !varAppLoaded,
    Concurrent(
        Refresh('RAMS Planner Headers'),
        Refresh('RAMS Evaluation Table'),
        Refresh('RACE - RAMS Planner'),
        Refresh('RAMS Hazards Checklist')
    )
);

ClearCollect(
    colEvalIndex,
    ShowColumns(
        'RAMS Evaluation Table',
        ID,
        EvaluationID,
        PlannerID,
        PlannerRowID,
        Collaborators,
        LastSavedByEmail,
        EvaluatorEmailAddress,
        'Created By'
    )
);



// ================================
// 5. LOAD STATIC TEMPLATES
// ================================
If(
    IsEmpty(colRAMSTemplate),
    ClearCollect(
        colRAMSTemplate,
        { num: 1,  desc: "Work at Height - Hierarchy of control applied, rescue plan?" },
        { num: 2,  desc: "Ground Works – Service drawings, exclusion zones, monitoring (noise/vibration)?" },
        { num: 3,  desc: "Mobile Plant – Segregation/exclusion/competent operator(s)?" },
        { num: 4,  desc: "Excavations - Access/egress, designed protection, rescue plan?" },
        { num: 5,  desc: "Power Tools – Correct selection, competent users?" },
        { num: 6,  desc: "Lifting Operations - Lift Plan, competent person, certified accessories?" },
        { num: 7,  desc: "Dangerous Energy – LOTO, authorised person, competent persons?" },
        { num: 8,  desc: "Manual Handling – Hierarchy of control applied, trained operatives?" },
        { num: 9,  desc: "Respiratory Risk – Engineered controls, ventilation, RPE?" },
        { num: 10, desc: "Occupational Health – Safety critical worker medical, air/HAV/noise monitoring?" },
        { num: 11, desc: "COSHH – Risk assessment, suitable storage, identification/information?" },
        { num: 12, desc: "Material Storage – Protected, secured?" },
        { num: 13, desc: "Falling Objects/Materials – Contained, storage, tethered tools?" },
        { num: 14, desc: "PPE/RPE – Correct selection, trained users?" },
        { num: 15, desc: "Access Ladders – Justified by risk assessment, designed/fixed?" },
        { num: 16, desc: "Emergency Arrangements – Fire/First Aid, competent persons?" },
        { num: 17, desc: "Access Equipment – Collective protection, inspected/tested?" },
        { num: 18, desc: "Supervision – Suitable levels, competent persons?" },
        { num: 19, desc: "Powered Access – Competent operators, harness, spotter?" },
        { num: 20, desc: "Temporary Works – Designed solutions, checks completed?" },
        { num: 21, desc: "Permit to Work – Hot works, Abrasive Wheels, Ladders?" },
        { num: 22, desc: "Confined Spaces – Entry, rescue plan, RPE/PPE?" }
    )
);

If(
    IsEmpty(colCOSHHTemplate),
    ClearCollect(
        colCOSHHTemplate,
        { num: 1,  desc: "Are all hazardous substances clearly listed?" },
        { num: 2,  desc: "Are Safety Data Sheets (SDS) provided and up to date?" },
        { num: 3,  desc: "Is the classification of each substance included?" },
        { num: 4,  desc: "Is the task involving the substance clearly described?" },
        { num: 5,  desc: "Are quantities, frequency, and duration specified?" },
        { num: 6,  desc: "Is the method of use explained?" },
        { num: 7,  desc: "Are potential routes of exposure identified?" },
        { num: 8,  desc: "Are exposed workers identified?" },
        { num: 9,  desc: "Is the level of risk evaluated realistically?" },
        { num: 10, desc: "Are control measures listed?" },
        { num: 11, desc: "Are controls appropriate and effective?" },
        { num: 12, desc: "Are spill and exposure procedures included?" },
        { num: 13, desc: "Is first aid guidance provided?" },
        { num: 14, desc: "Are emergency contacts and equipment identified?" }
    )
);


// ================================
// 6. DEEP LINKING LOGIC (SharePoint item ID)
// Example link parameter: ?EvalItemID=123
//
// IMPORTANT:
// Do NOT preload hazards here.
// Hazards are loaded in RAMS Evaluation Checklist.OnVisible
// only after varCanViewEvaluation = true.
// ================================
If(
    !IsBlank(Param("EvalItemID")),

    // 1. Resolve SharePoint item ID from deep link
    Set(
        varResolvedEvalItemID,
        Value(Param("EvalItemID"))
    );

    // 2. Load only the evaluation record
    Set(
        varEvalRec,
        LookUp(
            'RAMS Evaluation Table',
            ID = varResolvedEvalItemID
        )
    );

    Set(
        varEvalRecordReady,
        !IsBlank(varEvalRec)
    );

    // 3. If evaluation exists, restore only basic context
    If(
        !IsBlank(varEvalRec),

        Set(gblEvalItemID, varEvalRec.ID);
        Set(gblActiveEvaluationID, varEvalRec.EvaluationID);
        Set(gblPlannerID, varEvalRec.PlannerID);
        Set(gblPlannerRowID, varEvalRec.PlannerRowID);

        // Do not mark checklist as preloaded because hazards are not loaded here
        Set(varChecklistPreloaded, false);
        Set(varLoadedHazardsEvalID, Blank());
        Set(varLoadedCOSHHEvalID, Blank());

        // Basic collaborator check only.
        // Full view authorization happens in RAMS Evaluation Checklist.OnVisible.
        Set(
            varIsCollaborator,
            varIsAppAdmin || varIsHS 
            ||
            CountIf(
                colUserEmails As ue,
                ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(varEvalRec.LastSavedByEmail, ""))
            ) > 0
            ||
            CountIf(
                colUserEmails As ue,
                ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(varEvalRec.EvaluatorEmailAddress, ""))
            ) > 0
            ||
            CountIf(
                colUserEmails As ue,
                ue.Email =
                    Lower(
                        Coalesce(
                            varEvalRec.'Created By'.Email,
                            ""
                        )
                    )
            ) > 0
            ||
            !IsBlank(
                LookUp(
                    varEvalRec.Collaborators As collab,
                    CountIf(
                        colUserEmails As ue,
                        ue.Email =
                            Lower(
                                Coalesce(
                                    collab.Email,
                                    Substitute(
                                        collab.Claims,
                                        "i:0#.f|membership|",
                                        ""
                                    )
                                )
                            )
                    ) > 0
                )
            )
        );

        Set(
            varChecklistEditable,
            varIsCollaborator
        )
    )
);

// ================================
// 7. LIGHTWEIGHT LOCAL COLLECTIONS
// ================================
ClearCollect(
    colRecentActivities,
    ShowColumns(
        'RACE - RAMS Planner',
        PlannerID
    )
);

Set(varHSReps, Blank());



// ================================
// 8. FINAL FLAGS
// ================================
Set(varAppLoaded, true);



StartScreen:
If(
    !IsBlank(Param("EvalItemID")),
    'RAMS Evaluation Checklist',
    'RAMS Home'
)


Screens:
  RAMS Home:
    Properties:
      Fill: =RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)

        =// Refresh only when needed

        ClearCollect(

                PlannerID

        ClearCollect(
            colHomeEvalIndex,
            'RAMS Evaluation Table'
        );



            Set(varRefreshActivities, false)
        ;
        ClearCollect(
            colPlannerHeaders,
            SortByColumns(
                'RAMS Planner Headers',
                "Modified",
                SortOrder.Descending
            )
        );
        // Reset UI
        ClearCollect(colHSValidatorsEmails, {Value: ""});
        Clear(colHSValidatorsEmails);
        ClearCollect(
            colAccessiblePlannerHeaders,
            AddColumns(
                Filter(
                    colPlannerHeaders As hdr,
                    varIsAppAdmin
                    ||
                    CountIf(
                        colUserEmails,
                        Email = Lower(hdr.'Prepared By Email')
                    ) > 0
                    ||
                    !IsBlank(
                        LookUp(
                            hdr.ProjectManagers As pm,
                            CountIf(
                                colUserEmails As ue,
                                ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(pm.Email, ""))
                            ) > 0
                        )
                    )
                    ||
                    !IsBlank(
                        LookUp(
                            hdr.HSValidators As hs,
                            CountIf(
                                colUserEmails As ue,
                                ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(hs.Email, ""))
                            ) > 0
                        )
                    )
                    ||
                    !IsBlank(
                        LookUp(
                            colHomeEvalIndex As eval,
                            eval.PlannerID = hdr.PlannerID
                            &&
                            !IsBlank(
                                LookUp(
                                    eval.Collaborators As collab,
                                    CountIf(
                                        colUserEmails As ue,
                                        ue.Email =
                                            Lower(
                                                Coalesce(
                                                    collab.Email,

                                                        ""
                                                    )
                                                )
                                            )
                                    ) > 0
                                )
                            )
                        )
                    )

                    {
                        _n: Coalesce(
                            'Created By'.DisplayName,
                            'Prepared By',
                            'Prepared By Email',
                            ""
                        )

                        IsBlank(_n),
                        "",
                        Trim(
                            Left(
                                _n,
                                Find(
                                    "(",
                                    _n & "("
                                ) - 1
                            )
                        )
                    )
                )
            )
        );
        Reset(cmbCreatedbyFilter);
        Reset(cmbTitleFilter);
        Reset(cmbTradeFilter);
        UpdateContext({ showDrawer: false });
        Set(varLoading, false);
        Set(varTableMinWidth, 1350);

          Control: GroupContainer@1.5.0
          Variant: ManualLayout
          Properties:
            DropShadow: =DropShadow.None
            Fill: =RGBA(241, 245, 249, 1)
            Height: =Parent.Height - conNavbar_1.Height
            Width: =Parent.Width
            Y: =conNavbar_1.Height
          Children:
            - conMainArea_1:
                Control: GroupContainer@1.5.0
                Variant: ManualLayout
                Properties:
                  BorderColor: =RGBA(143, 102, 148, 1)
                  BorderThickness: =0.3
                  Fill: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                  Height: =Parent.Height - 40
                  RadiusBottomLeft: =0
                  RadiusBottomRight: =0
                  RadiusTopLeft: =10
                  RadiusTopRight: =10
                  Width: =Parent.Width - conSidebar_1.Width -30
                  X: =conSidebar_1.Width +15
                  Y: =20
                Children:
                  - galGallery:
                      Control: Gallery@2.15.0
                      Variant: BrowseLayout_Flexible_SocialFeed_ver5.0
                      Properties:
                        BorderColor: =RGBA(214, 221, 224, 1)

                                  hdr.Title = cmbTitleFilter.Selected.Value

Screens:
  RAMS Planner:
    Properties:
      Fill: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
      LoadingSpinnerColor: =RGBA(0, 120, 212, 1)
      OnVisible: |-
        =// RAMS Planner — OnVisible

        // ✅ 0) Always start in a clean, loading state
        Set(varSelectedItem, Blank());
        UpdateContext({ varLoading: true });

        // Optional: reset access flags while loading
        Set(varIsCurrentPlannerPM, false);
        Set(varIsCurrentPlannerHS, false);
        Set(varIsCurrentPlannerPreparedBy, false);
        Set(varIsCurrentPlannerCollaborator, false);
        Set(varCanViewPlanner, false);
        Set(varCanEditPlannerActivity, false);

        // ✅ Preserve return row (if any)
        Set(varReturnPlannerRowID, Value(paramReturnPlannerRowID));

        // ✅ 1) Set layout constants
        Set(varColRowID, 0.03);
        Set(varColActivity, 0.14);
        Set(varColRisk, 0.06);
        Set(varColDate, 0.055);
        Set(varColType, 0.05);
        Set(varColRequired, 0.06);
        Set(varColDocRev, 0.045);
        Set(varColHSTeam, 0.04);
        Set(varColActions, 0.03);

        // ✅ 2) Resolve active planner header
        If(
            !IsBlank(paramHeaderRecord),

            // Branch A: header record passed directly
            Set(varSelectedPlannerID, paramHeaderRecord.PlannerID);
            Set(varPlannerHeader, paramHeaderRecord);
            Set(varCurrentProjectName, paramHeaderRecord.'Project Name'.Value);
            Set(varCurrentRAMSTitle, paramHeaderRecord.Title);
            Set(varCurrentCreatedBy, paramHeaderRecord.'Prepared By');
            Set(varCurrentHSRep, paramHeaderRecord.HSValidators);
            Set(varCurrentTrade, paramHeaderRecord.'Trade - Package'.Value);
            Set(varCurrentPManagers, paramHeaderRecord.ProjectManagers),

            // Branch B: fallback by PlannerID
            Set(
                varSelectedPlannerID,
                paramPlannerID
            );

            Set(
                varPlannerHeader,
                LookUp(
                    'RAMS Planner Headers',
                    PlannerID = varSelectedPlannerID
                )
            );

            If(
                IsBlank(varPlannerHeader),
                Refresh('RAMS Planner Headers');
                Set(
                    varPlannerHeader,
                    LookUp(
                        'RAMS Planner Headers',
                        PlannerID = varSelectedPlannerID
                    )
                )
            );

            If(
                !IsBlank(varPlannerHeader),
                Set(varCurrentProjectName, varPlannerHeader.'Project Name'.Value);
                Set(varCurrentRAMSTitle, varPlannerHeader.Title);
                Set(varCurrentCreatedBy, varPlannerHeader.'Prepared By');
                Set(varCurrentHSRep, varPlannerHeader.HSValidators);
                Set(varCurrentTrade, varPlannerHeader.'Trade - Package'.Value);
                Set(varCurrentPManagers, varPlannerHeader.ProjectManagers)
            )
        );

        // ✅ 3) PM access
        Set(
            varIsCurrentPlannerPM,
            !IsBlank(
                LookUp(
                    varCurrentPManagers As pm,
                    CountIf(
                        colUserEmails As ue,
                        ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(pm.Email, ""))
                    ) > 0
                )
            )
        );

        // ✅ 4) H&S access
        Set(
            varIsCurrentPlannerHS,
            !IsBlank(
                LookUp(
                    varCurrentHSRep As hs,
                    CountIf(
                        colUserEmails As ue,
                        ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(hs.Email, ""))
                    ) > 0
                )
            )
        );

        // ✅ 5) Load planner rows and planner-specific evaluation index
        Concurrent(
            ClearCollect(
                colPlannerRows,
                AddColumns(
                    Filter(
                        'RACE - RAMS Planner',
                        PlannerID = varSelectedPlannerID
                    ),
                    '_OriginalLastModifiedOn',
                    LastModifiedDate
                )
            ),

            ClearCollect(
                colPlannerEvalIndex,
                Filter(
                    'RAMS Evaluation Table',
                    PlannerID = varSelectedPlannerID
                )
            )
        );

        // ✅ 6) Compatibility collection
        // Keeps older controls that still reference colEvalIndex working.
        ClearCollect(
            colEvalIndex,
            colPlannerEvalIndex
        );

        // ✅ 7) Prepared By access
        Set(
            varIsCurrentPlannerPreparedBy,
            CountIf(
                colUserEmails,
                Email = Lower(Coalesce(varPlannerHeader.'Prepared By Email', ""))
            ) > 0
        );

        // ✅ 8) Planner-level collaborator access
        Set(
            varIsCurrentPlannerCollaborator,
            !IsBlank(
                LookUp(
                    colPlannerEvalIndex As eval,
                    !IsBlank(
                        LookUp(
                            eval.Collaborators As collab,
                            CountIf(
                                colUserEmails As ue,
                                ue.Email =
                                    Lower(
                                        Coalesce(
                                            collab.Email,
                                            Substitute(
                                                collab.Claims,
                                                "i:0#.f|membership|",
                                                ""
                                            )
                                        )
                                    )
                            ) > 0
                        )
                    )
                )
            )
        );

        // ✅ 9) Final planner view access
        Set(
            varCanViewPlanner,
            varIsAppAdmin
            ||
            varIsCurrentPlannerPM
            ||
            varIsCurrentPlannerHS
            ||
            varIsCurrentPlannerPreparedBy
            ||
            varIsCurrentPlannerCollaborator
        );

        // ✅ 10) Final planner edit access
        Set(
            varCanEditPlannerActivity,
            varCanViewPlanner
        );

        // ✅ 11) Done
        UpdateContext({ varLoading: false });
    Children:

            - RefreshPlannerBtn:
                Control: Button@0.0.45
                Properties:
                  Align: =Align.Center
                  Appearance: ='ButtonCanvas.Appearance'.Secondary
                  BorderColor: =RGBA(180, 214, 250, 1)
                  BorderThickness: =1
                  DisplayMode: |
                    =
                    If(
                        varLoading || varIsSaving,
                        DisplayMode.Disabled,
                        DisplayMode.Edit
                    )
                  Font: =Font.'Lato Light'
                  FontColor: =RGBA(15, 84, 140, 1)
                  FontSize: =If(ScreenSize.Medium,12,13)
                  FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
                  Height: =30
                  Icon: ="ArrowSync"
                  IconStyle: ='ButtonCanvas.IconStyle'.Filled
                  OnSelect: |-
                    =// ================================
                    // RAMS Planner - Refresh Button
                    // Refreshes planner rows, evaluation index, header, roles, and access flags
                    // ================================

                    If(
                        CountIf(
                            Gallery1_2.AllItems,
                            'changed?_2'.Checked
                        ) > 0
                        ||
                        (
                            varShowNewRecord
                            &&
                            !IsBlank(Activities_4.Value)
                        ),

                        Notify(
                            "You have unsaved changes. Please save or cancel before refreshing.",
                            NotificationType.Warning,
                            2500
                        ),

                        UpdateContext({ varLoading: true });

                        // 1. Refresh required SharePoint lists
                        Concurrent(
                            Refresh('RACE - RAMS Planner'),
                            Refresh('RAMS Evaluation Table'),
                            Refresh('RAMS Planner Headers')
                        );

                        // 2. Reload active planner header
                        Set(
                            varPlannerHeader,
                            LookUp(
                                'RAMS Planner Headers',
                                PlannerID = varSelectedPlannerID
                            )
                        );

                        // 3. Restore header context
                        If(
                            !IsBlank(varPlannerHeader),
                            Set(varCurrentProjectName, varPlannerHeader.'Project Name'.Value);
                            Set(varCurrentRAMSTitle, varPlannerHeader.Title);
                            Set(varCurrentCreatedBy, varPlannerHeader.'Prepared By');
                            Set(varCurrentHSRep, varPlannerHeader.HSValidators);
                            Set(varCurrentTrade, varPlannerHeader.'Trade - Package'.Value);
                            Set(varCurrentPManagers, varPlannerHeader.ProjectManagers)
                        );

                        // 4. Recalculate PM role
                        Set(
                            varIsCurrentPlannerPM,
                            !IsBlank(
                                LookUp(
                                    varCurrentPManagers As pm,
                                    CountIf(
                                        colUserEmails As ue,
                                        ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(pm.Email, ""))
                                    ) > 0
                                )
                            )
                        );

                        // 5. Recalculate H&S role
                        Set(
                            varIsCurrentPlannerHS,
                            !IsBlank(
                                LookUp(
                                    varCurrentHSRep As hs,
                                    CountIf(
                                        colUserEmails As ue,
                                        ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(hs.Email, ""))
                                    ) > 0
                                )
                            )
                        );

                        // 6. Reload planner rows and current-planner evaluation index
                        Concurrent(
                            ClearCollect(
                                colPlannerRows,
                                AddColumns(
                                    Filter(
                                        'RACE - RAMS Planner',
                                        PlannerID = varSelectedPlannerID
                                    ),
                                    '_OriginalLastModifiedOn',
                                    LastModifiedDate
                                )
                            ),

                            ClearCollect(
                                colPlannerEvalIndex,
                                Filter(
                                    'RAMS Evaluation Table',
                                    PlannerID = varSelectedPlannerID
                                )
                            )
                        );

                        // 7. Compatibility collection
                        // Keeps older controls that still reference colEvalIndex working.
                        ClearCollect(
                            colEvalIndex,
                            colPlannerEvalIndex
                        );

                        // 8. Recalculate Prepared By access
                        Set(
                            varIsCurrentPlannerPreparedBy,
                            CountIf(
                                colUserEmails,
                                Email = Lower(Coalesce(varPlannerHeader.'Prepared By Email', ""))
                            ) > 0
                        );

                        // 9. Recalculate planner-level collaborator access
                        Set(
                            varIsCurrentPlannerCollaborator,
                            !IsBlank(
                                LookUp(
                                    colPlannerEvalIndex As eval,
                                    !IsBlank(
                                        LookUp(
                                            eval.Collaborators As collab,
                                            CountIf(
                                                colUserEmails As ue,
                                                ue.Email =
                                                    Lower(
                                                        Coalesce(
                                                            collab.Email,
                                                            Substitute(
                                                                collab.Claims,
                                                                "i:0#.f|membership|",
                                                                ""
                                                            )
                                                        )
                                                    )
                                            ) > 0
                                        )
                                    )
                                )
                            )
                        );

                        // 10. Recalculate final view access
                        Set(
                            varCanViewPlanner,
                            varIsAppAdmin
                            ||
                            varIsCurrentPlannerPM
                            ||
                            varIsCurrentPlannerHS
                            ||
                            varIsCurrentPlannerPreparedBy
                            ||
                            varIsCurrentPlannerCollaborator
                        );

                        // 11. Recalculate final edit access
                        Set(
                            varCanEditPlannerActivity,
                            varCanViewPlanner
                        );

                        // 12. Clear selected row to avoid stale edit state
                        Set(varSelectedItem, Blank());
                        Set(varShowNewRecord, false);

                        UpdateContext({ varLoading: false });

                        Notify(
                            "Planner refreshed successfully.",
                            NotificationType.Success,
                            1000
                        )
                    )
                  Text: ="""Refresh"""
                  VerticalAlign: =VerticalAlign.Middle
                  Width: =120
                  X: =
                  Y: =40

                        - NewSaveBtn_2:
                            Control: Button@0.0.45
                            Properties:
                              Appearance: ='ButtonCanvas.Appearance'.Transparent
                              DisplayMode: |-
                                =If(
                                    !IsBlank(Activities_4.Value) ,
                                    DisplayMode.Edit,
                                    DisplayMode.Disabled
                                )
                              Icon: ="Save"
                              Layout: ='ButtonCanvas.Layout'.IconOnly
                              LayoutMinHeight: =16
                              LayoutMinWidth: =16
                              OnSelect: |-
                                =// ================================
                                // Add new planner activity
                                // Includes error handling and local refresh
                                // ================================
                                UpdateContext({ varIsSaving: true });

                                With(
                                    {
                                        lastRow: First(
                                            Sort(
                                                Filter(
                                                    'RACE - RAMS Planner',
                                                    PlannerID = varSelectedPlannerID
                                                ),
                                                PlannerRowID,
                                                SortOrder.Descending
                                            )
                                        )
                                    },
                                    IfError(
                                        Set(
                                            varNewActivityRecord,
                                            Patch(
                                                'RACE - RAMS Planner',
                                                Defaults('RACE - RAMS Planner'),
                                                {
                                                    PlannerID: varSelectedPlannerID,

                                                    PlannerRowID: If(
                                                        IsBlank(lastRow),
                                                        1,
                                                        lastRow.PlannerRowID + 1
                                                    ),

                                                    Activities: Activities_4.Value,
                                                    'Risk Assessed': RiskAssessed_4.Selected,
                                                    Status: { Value: "New" },
                                                    'Activity Anticipated Start Date': AnticDate_4.SelectedDate,
                                                    'BYES-C Self Delivery or S/C ': Type_4.Selected,
                                                    Required: lblRiskAssessmentDecisionStatus.Text,
                                                    'Required by Date': RequiredDate_4.SelectedDate,
                                                    'Received Date': ReceivedDate_4.SelectedDate,
                                                    'Signed off Date': SignedOffDate_4.SelectedDate,
                                                    'Doc Revision': "0001",
                                                    LastModifiedDate: Now(),
                                                    LastModifiedBy: User().FullName,
                                                    'H&S Team Input (Medium/High Risk)': HSTeamRequired_4.Selected
                                                }
                                            )
                                        ),
                                        Notify(
                                            "Could not add activity: " & FirstError.Message,
                                            NotificationType.Error,
                                            3000
                                        )
                                    )
                                );

                                // Refresh SharePoint list
                                Refresh('RACE - RAMS Planner');

                                // Rebuild local planner rows
                                ClearCollect(
                                    colPlannerRows,
                                    AddColumns(
                                        Filter(
                                            'RACE - RAMS Planner',
                                            PlannerID = varSelectedPlannerID
                                        ),
                                        '_OriginalLastModifiedOn',
                                        LastModifiedDate
                                    )
                                );

                                // Reset input fields
                                Reset(Activities_4);
                                Reset(RiskAssessed_4);
                                Reset(AnticDate_4);
                                Reset(Type_4);
                                Reset(RequiredDate_4);
                                Reset(ReceivedDate_4);
                                Reset(SignedOffDate_4);
                                Reset(DocRevision_4);
                                Reset(HSTeamRequired_4);

                                // Close new row
                                Set(varShowNewRecord, false);

                                // Keep the new row visible but NOT editable
                                Set(varSelectedItem, Blank());

                                UpdateContext({ varIsSaving: false });

                                If(
                                    !IsBlank(varNewActivityRecord),
                                    Notify(
                                        "New activity added successfully",
                                        NotificationType.Success,
                                        1000
                                    )
                                );
                              Width: =varColActions*Parent.Width

                              - EvalButton:
                                  Control: Button@0.0.45
                                  Properties:
                                    AccessibleLabel: |-
                                      =Switch(
                                          Self.Text,
                                          "Start Evaluation",
                                          "No evaluation exists yet. Click to start one.",

                                          "Open Evaluation",
                                          "Open the existing evaluation checklist.",

                                          "Request Access",
                                          "You are not currently added to this evaluated activity.",

                                          ""
                                      )
                                    Appearance: ='ButtonCanvas.Appearance'.Secondary
                                    BorderColor: |-
                                      =With(
                                          {
                                              _text: Self.Text
                                          },
                                          Switch(
                                              _text,

                                              "Start Evaluation",
                                              RGBA(219, 234, 254, 1),

                                              "Open Evaluation",
                                              RGBA(220, 252, 231, 1),

                                              "Open PDF",
                                              RGBA(224, 231, 255, 1),

                                              "PDF Pending",
                                              RGBA(254, 243, 199, 1),

                                              "Request Access",
                                              RGBA(254, 249, 195, 1),

                                              "Rejected",
                                              RGBA(254, 226, 226, 1),

                                              RGBA(241, 245, 249, 1)
                                          )
                                      )
                                    BorderRadius: =5
                                    BorderStyle: =BorderStyle.Solid
                                    BorderThickness: |-
                                      =If(
                                          Self.Text in ["Request Access", "PDF Pending"],
                                          1,
                                          0.5
                                      )
                                    Font: =Font.'Lato Light'
                                    FontColor: |-
                                      =With(
                                          {
                                              _text: Self.Text
                                          },
                                          Switch(
                                              _text,

                                              "Start Evaluation",
                                              RGBA(30, 64, 175, 1),

                                              "Open Evaluation",
                                              RGBA(21, 128, 61, 1),

                                              "Open PDF",
                                              RGBA(67, 56, 202, 1),

                                              "PDF Pending",
                                              RGBA(146, 64, 14, 1),

                                              "Request Access",
                                              RGBA(133, 77, 14, 1),

                                              "Rejected",
                                              RGBA(153, 27, 27, 1),

                                              RGBA(51, 65, 85, 1)
                                          )
                                      )
                                    FontSize: =9
                                    FontWeight: =FontWeight.Bold
                                    Icon: ="ArrowRight"
                                    IconStyle: ='ButtonCanvas.IconStyle'.Filled
                                    Layout: ='ButtonCanvas.Layout'.IconAfter
                                    LayoutMinHeight: =16
                                    LayoutMinWidth: =16
                                    OnSelect: |-
                                      =// ================================
                                      // EvalButton - Hybrid access + completed PDF + rejected behaviour
                                      // - No evaluation exists: Start Evaluation
                                      // - Rejected evaluation: show Create as New guidance
                                      // - Completed evaluation + PDF link: Open PDF
                                      // - Completed evaluation + no PDF link: PDF Pending message
                                      // - In-progress evaluation + access: Open Checklist
                                      // - In-progress evaluation + no access: Request Access message
                                      // ================================

                                      With(
                                          {
                                              _eval: LookUp(
                                                  colPlannerEvalIndex,
                                                  EvaluationID = ThisItem.PlannerID & "-" & Text(ThisItem.PlannerRowID)
                                              )
                                          },

                                          With(
                                              {
                                                  _isRejected:
                                                      !IsBlank(_eval)
                                                      &&
                                                      _eval.EvaluationStatus.Value = "Rejected",

                                                  _isCompleted:
                                                      !IsBlank(_eval)
                                                      &&
                                                      (
                                                          Coalesce(_eval.Completed, false)
                                                          ||
                                                          _eval.EvaluationStatus.Value in [
                                                              "Accepted",
                                                              "Not Required",
                                                              "Complete"
                                                          ]
                                                      ),

                                                  _userCanOpenExistingEval:
                                                      IsBlank(_eval)
                                                      ||
                                                      varIsAppAdmin
                                                      ||
                                                      varIsCurrentPlannerPM
                                                      ||
                                                      varIsCurrentPlannerHS
                                                      ||
                                                      varIsCurrentPlannerPreparedBy
                                                      ||
                                                      CountIf(
                                                          colUserEmails As ue,
                                                          ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(_eval.LastSavedByEmail, ""))
                                                      ) > 0
                                                      ||
                                                      CountIf(
                                                          colUserEmails As ue,
                                                          ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(_eval.EvaluatorEmailAddress, ""))
                                                      ) > 0
                                                      ||
                                                      CountIf(
                                                          colUserEmails As ue,
                                                          ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(_eval.'Created By'.Email, ""))
                                                      ) > 0
                                                      ||
                                                      !IsBlank(
                                                          LookUp(
                                                              _eval.Collaborators As collab,
                                                              CountIf(
                                                                  colUserEmails As ue,
                                                                  ue.Email =
                                                                      Lower(
                                                                          Coalesce(
                                                                              collab.Email,
                                                                              Substitute(
                                                                                  collab.Claims,
                                                                                  "i:0#.f|membership|",
                                                                                  ""
                                                                              )
                                                                          )
                                                                      )
                                                              ) > 0
                                                          )
                                                      )
                                              },

                                              If(
                                                  // ================================
                                                  // A) Rejected evaluation
                                                  // ================================
                                                  _isRejected,

                                                  Notify(
                                                      "This evaluation has been rejected. Please use the Create as New button on the right to create a new version.",
                                                      NotificationType.Warning,
                                                      4000
                                                  ),

                                                  // ================================
                                                  // B) Completed evaluation: open PDF instead of checklist
                                                  // ================================
                                                  _isCompleted,

                                                  If(
                                                      IsBlank(_eval.FinalPDFLink),
                                                      Notify(
                                                          "Final PDF is not available yet. Please refresh later or contact the Project Manager.",
                                                          NotificationType.Warning,
                                                          2500
                                                      ),
                                                      Launch(_eval.FinalPDFLink)
                                                  ),

                                                  // ================================
                                                  // C) Existing in-progress evaluation but user has no access
                                                  // ================================
                                                  !IsBlank(_eval) && !_userCanOpenExistingEval,

                                                  Notify(
                                                      "You are not currently a collaborator on this activity. Please ask the Project Manager or H&S Validator to add you as a collaborator.",
                                                      NotificationType.Warning,
                                                      4000
                                                  ),

                                                  // ================================
                                                  // D) Existing logic for Start/Open Evaluation
                                                  // ================================

                                                  // 1) Refresh only Evaluation Table to get latest lock/status
                                                  Refresh('RAMS Evaluation Table');

                                                  // 2) Set global identities from clicked planner row
                                                  Set(gblPlannerID, ThisItem.PlannerID);
                                                  Set(gblPlannerRowID, ThisItem.PlannerRowID);

                                                  // 3) Build internal EvaluationID
                                                  Set(
                                                      gblActiveEvaluationID,
                                                      ThisItem.PlannerID & "-" & Text(ThisItem.PlannerRowID)
                                                  );

                                                  // 4) Resolve related records before navigation
                                                  With(
                                                      {
                                                          _evalRec: LookUp(
                                                              'RAMS Evaluation Table',
                                                              EvaluationID = gblActiveEvaluationID
                                                          ),
                                                          _plannerRow: LookUp(
                                                              'RACE - RAMS Planner',
                                                              PlannerID = gblPlannerID &&
                                                              PlannerRowID = gblPlannerRowID
                                                          ),
                                                          _plannerHeader: LookUp(
                                                              'RAMS Planner Headers',
                                                              PlannerID = gblPlannerID
                                                          )
                                                      },

                                                      // 5) Store resolved records / IDs
                                                      Set(varEvalRec, _evalRec);
                                                      Set(varPlannerRow, _plannerRow);
                                                      Set(varPlannerHeader, _plannerHeader);

                                                      Set(
                                                          gblEvalItemID,
                                                          If(
                                                              !IsBlank(_evalRec),
                                                              _evalRec.ID,
                                                              Blank()
                                                          )
                                                      );

                                                      Set(
                                                          varResolvedEvalItemID,
                                                          If(
                                                              !IsBlank(_evalRec),
                                                              _evalRec.ID,
                                                              Blank()
                                                          )
                                                      );

                                                      Set(varEvalRecordReady, !IsBlank(_evalRec));

                                                      // 6) Branch safely
                                                      If(
                                                          IsBlank(_evalRec),

                                                          // OPTION A — No evaluation exists yet
                                                          Set(varChecklistPreloaded, false);
                                                          NewForm(EvaluationTable_1);
                                                          Navigate(
                                                              'RAMS Evaluation Form',
                                                              ScreenTransition.Cover
                                                          ),

                                                          // OPTION B — Evaluation exists
                                                          If(
                                                              IsBlank(_plannerRow) || IsBlank(_plannerHeader),

                                                              Notify(
                                                                  "The evaluation exists, but the linked planner row or planner header could not be loaded.",
                                                                  NotificationType.Error,
                                                                  1000
                                                              ),

                                                              // 7) Check lock
                                                              Set(
                                                                  varIsLocked,
                                                                  !IsBlank(_evalRec.EditingBy)
                                                                  &&
                                                                  Lower(_evalRec.EditingBy) <> Lower(User().Email)
                                                                  &&
                                                                  DateDiff(
                                                                      _evalRec.EditingAt,
                                                                      Now(),
                                                                      TimeUnit.Minutes
                                                                  ) <= Coalesce(varEditLockMinutes, 30)
                                                              );

                                                              If(
                                                                  varIsLocked,

                                                                  Notify(
                                                                      "This evaluation is currently being edited by " &
                                                                      _evalRec.EditingBy &
                                                                      " (" &
                                                                      DateDiff(
                                                                          _evalRec.EditingAt,
                                                                          Now(),
                                                                          TimeUnit.Minutes
                                                                      ) &
                                                                      " mins ago)",
                                                                      NotificationType.Warning,
                                                                      2000
                                                                  ),

                                                                  // 8) Not locked: preload RAMS hazards only
                                                                  ClearCollect(
                                                                      colCurrentHazards,
                                                                      SortByColumns(
                                                                          AddColumns(
                                                                              Filter(
                                                                                  'RAMS Hazards Checklist',
                                                                                  EvaluationID = gblActiveEvaluationID &&
                                                                                  IsCOSHH = false
                                                                              ),
                                                                              'IsDirty',
                                                                              false
                                                                          ),
                                                                          "HazardsNo",
                                                                          SortOrder.Ascending
                                                                      )
                                                                  );

                                                                  // 9) Claim / refresh evaluation lock
                                                                  Set(
                                                                      varEvalRec,
                                                                      Patch(
                                                                          'RAMS Evaluation Table',
                                                                          { ID: _evalRec.ID },
                                                                          {
                                                                              EditingBy: User().Email,
                                                                              EditingAt: Now()
                                                                          }
                                                                      )
                                                                  );

                                                                  // 10) Set preload flags for faster Checklist OnVisible
                                                                  Set(varChecklistPreloaded, true);
                                                                  Set(varLoadedHazardsEvalID, gblActiveEvaluationID);

                                                                  // Keep COSHH unloaded until COSHH screen opens
                                                                  Set(varLoadedCOSHHEvalID, Blank());

                                                                  // 11) Navigate to checklist
                                                                  Navigate(
                                                                      'RAMS Evaluation Checklist',
                                                                      ScreenTransition.Cover
                                                                  )
                                                              )
                                                          )
                                                      )
                                                  )
                                              )
                                          )
                                      )
                                    Text: |-
                                      =With(
                                          {
                                              _eval: LookUp(
                                                  colPlannerEvalIndex,
                                                  EvaluationID = ThisItem.PlannerID & "-" & Text(ThisItem.PlannerRowID)
                                              )
                                          },
                                          With(
                                              {
                                                  _isRejected:
                                                      !IsBlank(_eval)
                                                      &&
                                                      _eval.EvaluationStatus.Value = "Rejected",

                                                  _isCompleted:
                                                      !IsBlank(_eval)
                                                      &&
                                                      (
                                                          Coalesce(_eval.Completed, false)
                                                          ||
                                                          _eval.EvaluationStatus.Value in [
                                                              "Accepted",
                                                              "Not Required",
                                                              "Complete"
                                                          ]
                                                      ),

                                                  _userCanOpenExistingEval:
                                                      IsBlank(_eval)
                                                      ||
                                                      varIsAppAdmin
                                                      ||
                                                      varIsCurrentPlannerPM
                                                      ||
                                                      varIsCurrentPlannerHS
                                                      ||
                                                      varIsCurrentPlannerPreparedBy
                                                      ||
                                                      CountIf(
                                                          colUserEmails As ue,
                                                          ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(_eval.LastSavedByEmail, ""))
                                                      ) > 0
                                                      ||
                                                      CountIf(
                                                          colUserEmails As ue,
                                                          ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(_eval.EvaluatorEmailAddress, ""))
                                                      ) > 0
                                                      ||
                                                      CountIf(
                                                          colUserEmails As ue,
                                                          ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(_eval.'Created By'.Email, ""))
                                                      ) > 0
                                                      ||
                                                      !IsBlank(
                                                          LookUp(
                                                              _eval.Collaborators As collab,
                                                              CountIf(
                                                                  colUserEmails As ue,
                                                                  ue.Email =
                                                                      Lower(
                                                                          Coalesce(
                                                                              collab.Email,
                                                                              Substitute(
                                                                                  collab.Claims,
                                                                                  "i:0#.f|membership|",
                                                                                  ""
                                                                              )
                                                                          )
                                                                      )
                                                              ) > 0
                                                          )
                                                      )
                                              },
                                              If(
                                                  IsBlank(_eval),
                                                  "Start Evaluation",

                                                  _isRejected,
                                                  "Rejected",

                                                  _isCompleted && !IsBlank(_eval.FinalPDFLink),
                                                  "Open PDF",

                                                  _isCompleted && IsBlank(_eval.FinalPDFLink),
                                                  "PDF Pending",

                                                  _userCanOpenExistingEval,
                                                  "Open Evaluation",

                                                  "Request Access"
                                              )
                                          )
                                      )
                                    Width: =100
                              - DuplicateButton:
                                  Control: Button@0.0.45
                                  Properties:

                                    BorderColor: =RGBA(214, 221, 224, 1)
                                    BorderStyle: =BorderStyle.Solid
                                    BorderThickness: =1

                                      =If(
                                          ThisItem.EvaluationStatus.Value = "Rejected",
                                          DisplayMode.Edit,
                                          If(
                                              ThisItem.EvaluationStatus.Value = "Rejected - Duplicated",
                                              DisplayMode.Disabled,
                                              ""
                                          )
                                      )

                                    FontSize: =10
                                    FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
                                    Icon: |-
                                      =If(
                                          ThisItem.Status.Value = "Rejected",
                                          "Add",
                                          If(
                                              ThisItem.Status.Value = "Rejected - Duplicated",
                                              "Copy",
                                              ""
                                          )
                                      )

                                    LayoutMinWidth: =16
                                    OnSelect: |-
                                      =UpdateContext({ varLoading: true });

                                      With(
                                          {
                                              // ✅ ALWAYS resolve to clean SharePoint record
                                              _currentRecord:
                                                  LookUp(
                                                      'RACE - RAMS Planner',
                                                      ID = ThisItem.ID
                                                  ),

                                              _lastRow:
                                                  First(
                                                      Sort(
                                                          Filter(
                                                              'RACE - RAMS Planner',
                                                              PlannerID = ThisItem.PlannerID
                                                          ),
                                                          PlannerRowID,
                                                          SortOrder.Descending
                                                      )
                                                  )
                                          },

                                          // ✅ 1. Mark current as duplicated
                                          Patch(
                                              'RACE - RAMS Planner',
                                              _currentRecord,
                                              {
                                                  EvaluationStatus: { Value: "Rejected - Duplicated" }
                                              }
                                          );

                                          // ✅ 2. Create new revision
                                          Patch(
                                              'RACE - RAMS Planner',
                                              Defaults('RACE - RAMS Planner'),
                                              {
                                                  PlannerID: _currentRecord.PlannerID,

                                                  PlannerRowID:
                                                      If(
                                                          IsBlank(_lastRow),
                                                          1,
                                                          _lastRow.PlannerRowID + 1
                                                      ),

                                                  Activities:
                                                      _currentRecord.Activities &
                                                      " (Rev " & (Value(_currentRecord.'Doc Revision') + 1) & ")",

                                                  'Risk Assessed': _currentRecord.'Risk Assessed',

                                                  'BYES-C Self Delivery or S/C ':
                                                      _currentRecord.'BYES-C Self Delivery or S/C ',

                                                  'Doc Revision':
                                                      Text(
                                                          Value(_currentRecord.'Doc Revision') + 1,
                                                          "[$-en-GB]0000"
                                                      ),

                                                  Status: { Value: "New" },

                                                  'Activity Anticipated Start Date':
                                                      _currentRecord.'Activity Anticipated Start Date',

                                                  'Required by Date':
                                                      _currentRecord.'Required by Date',

                                                  'H&S Team Input (Medium/High Risk)':
                                                      _currentRecord.'H&S Team Input (Medium/High Risk)'
                                              }
                                          )
                                      );

                                      // ✅ Refresh + rebuild snapshot (CRITICAL for your conflict logic)
                                      Refresh('RACE - RAMS Planner');
                                      ClearCollect(
                                          colPlannerRows,
                                          AddColumns(
                                              Filter(
                                                  'RACE - RAMS Planner',
                                                  PlannerID = varSelectedPlannerID
                                              ),
                                              '_OriginalLastModifiedOn',
                                              LastModifiedDate
                                          )
                                      );

                                      // ✅ 👉 ADD IT RIGHT HERE
                                      Set(
                                          varSelectedItem,
                                          Last(
                                              Sort(
                                                  colPlannerRows,
                                                  PlannerRowID,
                                                  SortOrder.Ascending
                                              )
                                          )
                                      );

                                      // ✅ Reset UI
                                      Set(varRefreshActivities, true);
                                      UpdateContext({ varLoading: false });

                                      Notify(
                                          "✅ New revision created successfully",
                                          NotificationType.Success,
                                          800
                                      );

                                      =If(
                                          ThisItem.EvaluationStatus.Value = "Rejected",
                                          "Create as New",
                                          If(
                                              ThisItem.EvaluationStatus.Value = "Rejected - Duplicated",
                                              "Duplicated",
                                              ""
                                          )

stroke-linejoin='round'/></g><g transform='translate(162.641 116)'><path d='M6.928,-12c0,0-13.856,8-13.856,8s0,16,0,16s13.856-8,13.856-8s0-16,0-16Z' fill='#fff' stroke='#000' stroke-width='3' stroke-linecap='round' stroke-linejoin='round'/></g><g transform='translate(155.713 104)'><path d='M0,-8c0,0-13.856,8-13.856,8C-13.856,0,0,8,0,8s13.856-8,13.856-8-13.856-8-13.856-8Z' fill='#fff' transform='translate(-128,-128)'><g transform='translate(121.072 132)'><path d='M-6.928,-12c0,0,13.856,8,13.856,8s0,16,0,16-13.856-8-13.856-8s0-16,0-16Z' fill='#fff' stroke='#000' stroke-width='3' stroke-linecap='round' stroke-linejoin='round'/></g><g transform='translate(134.928 132)'><path d='M6.928,-12c0,0-13.856,8-13.856,8s0,16,0,16s13.856-8,13.856-8s0-16,0-16Z' fill='#fff' stroke='#000' stroke-width='3' stroke-linecap='round' stroke-linejoin='round'/></g><g transform='translate(128 120)'><path d='M0,-8c0,0-13.856,8-13.856,8C-13.856,0,0,8,0,8s13.856-8,13.856-8-13.856-8-13.856-8Z'
