transform='translate(-135.33781,-207.03926)' fill='#d2dbed' stroke-width='0'/></g></mask></g><g id='eX8FLwRlbIY291_tr' transform='translate(11.12,29.9) rotate(-720)'></g></g></svg>
              ")
            PressedBorderColor: =RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)
            PressedFill: =RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)
            Visible: =varShowEvalLoader
            Width: =Parent.Width/2
            X: =(App.Width - Self.Width) / 2
            Y: =(App.Height - Self.Height) / 2
      - loadertext:
          Control: ModernText@1.0.0
          Group: evalloadergroup
          Properties:
            Align: =Align.Center
            Font: =Font.'Lato Light'
            FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
            Height: =40
            Size: =20
            Text: ="Preparing evaluation checklist..."
            Visible: =varShowEvalLoader
            Width: =Parent.Width
            Y: =60
      - tmrEvalPostSubmit:
          Control: Timer@2.1.0
          Properties:
            BorderColor: =RGBA(0, 120, 212, 1)
            BorderStyle: =BorderStyle.None
            Color: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
            DisabledBorderColor: =RGBA(242, 242, 241, 0)
            DisabledColor: =RGBA(161, 159, 157, 1)
            DisabledFill: =RGBA(242, 242, 241, 0)
            Duration: =300
            Fill: =RGBA(0, 120, 212, 1)
            Font: =Font.'Segoe UI'
            HoverBorderColor: =RGBA(16, 110, 190, 1)
            HoverColor: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
            HoverFill: =RGBA(16, 110, 190, 1)
            OnTimerEnd: |-
              =// ================================
              // Timer - Post Evaluation Submit Processing
              // Runs after loader has rendered.
              // ================================

              Set(varRunEvalPostSubmit, false);

              If(
                  !IsBlank(varSubmittedEval.EvaluationID),

                  With(
                      {
                          _newEval: varSubmittedEval,

                          _requiredStatus:
                              If(
                                  __prefill_RiskRating = "Low",
                                  "PM Approval",
                                  "H&S Acceptance"
                              ),

                          _plannerRow:
                              LookUp(
                                  'RACE - RAMS Planner',
                                  PlannerID = gblPlannerID &&
                                  PlannerRowID = Value(gblPlannerRowID)
                              ),

                          _plannerHeader:
                              LookUp(
                                  'RAMS Planner Headers',
                                  PlannerID = gblPlannerID
                              )
                      },

                      // ================================
                      // 1. UPDATE GLOBAL CONTEXT
                      // ================================
                      Set(gblEvalItemID, _newEval.ID);
                      Set(gblActiveEvaluationID, _newEval.EvaluationID);
                      Set(gblPlannerID, _newEval.PlannerID);
                      Set(gblPlannerRowID, _newEval.PlannerRowID);

                      // ================================
                      // 2. PATCH EVALUATION
                      // Preserve existing status on edit.
                      // Set In Progress only for new/blank status.
                      // ================================
                      Set(
                          varEvalRec,
                          Patch(
                              'RAMS Evaluation Table',
                              { ID: _newEval.ID },
                              {
                                  Status:
                                      If(
                                          __isNewEval || IsBlank(_newEval.Status.Value),
                                          { Value: "In Progress" },
                                          _newEval.Status
                                      ),

                                  ReviewLevelRequired: {
                                      '@odata.type': "#Microsoft.Azure.Connectors.SharePoint.SPListExpandedReference",
                                      Value: _requiredStatus
                                  },

                                  ActivityRiskRating: {
                                      '@odata.type': "#Microsoft.Azure.Connectors.SharePoint.SPListExpandedReference",
                                      Value: __prefill_RiskRating
                                  },

                                  ProjectName: varCurrentProjectName,
                                  LastSavedByEmail: User().Email,
                                  LastSavedOn: Now(),
                                  EditingBy: User().Email,
                                  EditingAt: Now()
                              }
                          )
                      );

                      // ================================
                      // 3. SYNC PLANNER ROW
                      // Do not regress status on edit.
                      // ================================
                      Set(
                          varPlannerRow,
                          Patch(
                              'RACE - RAMS Planner',
                              _plannerRow,
                              If(
                                  __isNewEval,
                                  {
                                      'Received Date': _newEval.RAMSReceivedDate,
                                      Status: { Value: "In Progress" }
                                  },
                                  {
                                      'Received Date': _newEval.RAMSReceivedDate
                                  }
                              )
                          )
                      );

                      Set(varPlannerHeader, _plannerHeader);

                      // ================================
                      // 4. SEED RAMS HAZARDS ONLY IF NONE EXIST
                      // COSHH is intentionally not seeded here.
                      // ================================
                      If(
                          IsBlank(
                              LookUp(
                                  'RAMS Hazards Checklist',
                                  EvaluationID = _newEval.EvaluationID &&
                                  IsCOSHH = false
                              )
                          ),

                          Clear(colUploadBuffer);

                          Collect(
                              colUploadBuffer,
                              ForAll(
                                  colRAMSTemplate,
                                  {
                                      Title: "Hazard Q" & num & " - " & _newEval.EvaluationID,
                                      EvaluationID: _newEval.EvaluationID,
                                      PlannerID: _newEval.PlannerID,
                                      PlannerRowID: Value(_newEval.PlannerRowID),
                                      HazardsNo: num,
                                      COSHHNumber: 0,
                                      HazardsIdentified: desc,
                                      IsCOSHH: false,
                                      SuitableControls: {
                                          '@odata.type': "#Microsoft.Azure.Connectors.SharePoint.SPListExpandedReference",
                                          Value: "Not Applicable"
                                      }
                                  }
                              )
                          );

                          Collect(
                              'RAMS Hazards Checklist',
                              colUploadBuffer
                          );

                          Clear(colUploadBuffer);

                          Set(
                              varEvalRec,
                              Patch(
                                  'RAMS Evaluation Table',
                                  { ID: varEvalRec.ID },
                                  {
                                      HasSeededHazards: true,
                                      EditingBy: User().Email,
                                      EditingAt: Now(),
                                      LastSavedByEmail: User().Email,
                                      LastSavedOn: Now()
                                  }
                              )
                          )
                      );

                      // ================================
                      // 5. LOAD RAMS HAZARDS LOCALLY
                      // ================================
                      ClearCollect(
                          colCurrentHazards,
                          SortByColumns(
                              AddColumns(
                                  Filter(
                                      'RAMS Hazards Checklist',
                                      EvaluationID = _newEval.EvaluationID &&
                                      IsCOSHH = false
                                  ),
                                  'IsDirty',
                                  false
                              ),
                              "HazardsNo",
                              SortOrder.Ascending
                          )
                      );

                      // ================================
                      // 6. SELF-HEAL SEEDED FLAG IF HAZARDS EXIST
                      // ================================
                      If(
                          !Coalesce(varEvalRec.HasSeededHazards, false) &&
                          !IsEmpty(colCurrentHazards),

                          Set(
                              varEvalRec,
                              Patch(
                                  'RAMS Evaluation Table',
                                  { ID: varEvalRec.ID },
                                  {
                                      HasSeededHazards: true,
                                      EditingBy: User().Email,
                                      EditingAt: Now(),
                                      LastSavedByEmail: User().Email,
                                      LastSavedOn: Now()
                                  }
                              )
                          )
                      );

                      // ================================
                      // 7. REFRESH PLANNER COLLECTION LOCALLY
                      // ================================
                      ClearCollect(
                          colPlannerRows,
                          AddColumns(
                              Filter(
                                  'RACE - RAMS Planner',
                                  PlannerID = gblPlannerID
                              ),
                              '_OriginalLastModifiedOn',
                              LastModifiedDate
                          )
                      );

                      // ================================
                      // 8. PRELOAD FLAGS FOR FAST CHECKLIST LOAD
                      // ================================
                      Set(varChecklistPreloaded, true);
                      Set(varLoadedHazardsEvalID, gblActiveEvaluationID);
                      Set(varLoadedCOSHHEvalID, Blank());

                      // ================================
                      // 9. STOP LOADER AND NAVIGATE
                      // ================================
                      Set(varShowEvalLoader, false);

                      Navigate(
                          'RAMS Evaluation Checklist',
                          ScreenTransition.Cover
                      )
                  ),

                  // ELSE: Submitted evaluation missing EvaluationID
                  Set(varShowEvalLoader, false);

                  Notify(
                      "Evaluation was saved, but the Evaluation ID could not be found.",
                      NotificationType.Error,
                      3000
                  )
              )
            PressedBorderColor: =RGBA(16, 110, 190, 1)
            PressedColor: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
            PressedFill: =RGBA(16, 110, 190, 1)
            Start: =varRunEvalPostSubmit
            Visible: =false
            X: =40
            Y: =40


Screens:
  RAMS Evaluation Checklist:
    Properties:
      Fill: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
      LoadingSpinnerColor: =RGBA(0, 120, 212, 1)
      OnVisible: |-
        =// ================================
        // RAMS Evaluation Checklist - OnVisible
        // Production-ready version
        // - Reuses preloaded records
        // - Explicit deep-link/view authorization
        // - Loads RAMS hazards only
        // - Does not load COSHH here
        // - Seeds RAMS only if missing
        // ================================


        // ================================
        // 1. RESET UI STATE
        // ================================
        Set(varErrorPopUp, false);
        Set(varErrorMessage, Blank());
        Set(varResolvedEvalItemID, Blank());

        UpdateContext({
            varLoading: true,
            varShowChecklist: false,
            varProgressCount: 0
        });

        Set(varHeaderRender, false);
        Set(varCommentRender, true);

        Set(varIsHS, false);
        Set(varIsPlannerPM, false);
        Set(varIsCollaborator, false);
        Set(varCanViewEvaluation, false);
        Set(varChecklistEditable, false);
        Set(varApprovalRole, Blank());
        Set(varHasEvaluationAttachment, false);
        Set(varLastSavedName, Blank());
        Set(varShowInspector, true);

        // Do not clear preloaded records unless this was not a preloaded navigation
        If(
            !varChecklistPreloaded,
            Set(varEvalRec, Blank());
            Set(varPlannerRow, Blank());
            Set(varPlannerHeader, Blank());
            Set(varPlannerRowRec, Blank())
        );


        // ================================
        // 2. RESOLVE EVALUATION ITEM ID
        // Internal navigation wins over deep link.
        // ================================
        Set(
            varResolvedEvalItemID,
            Coalesce(
                gblEvalItemID,
                If(
                    !IsBlank(Param("EvalItemID")),
                    Value(Param("EvalItemID")),
                    Blank()
                )
            )
        );


        // ================================
        // 3. GUARD: NO ITEM ID
        // ================================
        If(
            IsBlank(varResolvedEvalItemID) || varResolvedEvalItemID = 0,

            Set(
                varErrorMessage,
                "No evaluation item ID was found in the link or current session."
            );
            Set(varErrorPopUp, true);
            UpdateContext({
                varLoading: false,
                varShowChecklist: false
            }),

            // ELSE: Evaluation item ID exists

            // ================================
            // 4. LOAD EVALUATION ONLY IF NEEDED
            // ================================
            If(
                !varChecklistPreloaded
                ||
                IsBlank(varEvalRec)
                ||
                varEvalRec.ID <> varResolvedEvalItemID,

                Set(
                    varEvalRec,
                    LookUp(
                        'RAMS Evaluation Table',
                        ID = varResolvedEvalItemID
                    )
                )
            );

            // ================================
            // 5. GUARD: EVALUATION NOT FOUND
            // ================================
            If(
                IsBlank(varEvalRec),

                Set(
                    varErrorMessage,
                    "Evaluation record not found for item ID: " & Text(varResolvedEvalItemID)
                );
                Set(varErrorPopUp, true);
                UpdateContext({
                    varLoading: false,
                    varShowChecklist: false
                }),

                // ELSE: Evaluation found

                // ================================
                // 6. RESTORE GLOBAL CONTEXT
                // ================================
                Set(gblEvalItemID, varEvalRec.ID);
                Set(gblActiveEvaluationID, varEvalRec.EvaluationID);
                Set(gblPlannerID, varEvalRec.PlannerID);
                Set(gblPlannerRowID, varEvalRec.PlannerRowID);
                Set(varEvalRecordReady, true);

                // ================================
                // 7. LOAD PLANNER ROW ONLY IF NEEDED
                // ================================
                If(
                    IsBlank(varPlannerRow)
                    ||
                    varPlannerRow.PlannerID <> gblPlannerID
                    ||
                    Value(varPlannerRow.PlannerRowID) <> Value(gblPlannerRowID),

                    Set(
                        varPlannerRow,
                        LookUp(
                            'RACE - RAMS Planner',
                            PlannerID = gblPlannerID &&
                            PlannerRowID = Value(gblPlannerRowID)
                        )
                    )
                );

                // ================================
                // 8. LOAD PLANNER HEADER ONLY IF NEEDED
                // ================================
                If(
                    IsBlank(varPlannerHeader)
                    ||
                    varPlannerHeader.PlannerID <> varEvalRec.PlannerID,

                    Set(
                        varPlannerHeader,
                        LookUp(
                            'RAMS Planner Headers',
                            PlannerID = varEvalRec.PlannerID
                        )
                    )
                );

                // ================================
                // 9. GUARD: BROKEN CONTEXT
                // ================================
                If(
                    IsBlank(varPlannerRow) || IsBlank(varPlannerHeader),

                    Set(
                        varErrorMessage,
                        "The evaluation exists, but the planner details could not be loaded."
                    );
                    Set(varErrorPopUp, true);
                    UpdateContext({
                        varLoading: false,
                        varShowChecklist: false
                    }),

                    // ELSE: Planner context loaded

                    // ================================
                    // 10. CALCULATE ROLES BEFORE LOCK CLAIM
                    // This is important for deep-link security.
                    // ================================
                    Set(
                        varIsHS,
                        varIsAppAdmin
                        ||
                        !IsBlank(
                            LookUp(
                                varPlannerHeader.HSValidators As hs,
                                CountIf(
                                    colUserEmails As ue,
                                    ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(hs.Email, ""))
                                ) > 0
                            )
                        )
                    );

                    Set(
                        varIsPlannerPM,
                        !IsBlank(
                            LookUp(
                                varPlannerHeader.ProjectManagers As pm,
                                CountIf(
                                    colUserEmails As ue,
                                    ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(pm.Email, ""))
                                ) > 0
                            )
                        )
                    );

                    Set(
                        varIsCollaborator,
                        varIsAppAdmin
                        ||
                        CountIf(
                            colUserEmails As ue,
                            ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(varEvalRec.LastSavedByEmail, ""))
                        ) > 0
                        ||
                        CountIf(
                            colUserEmails As ue,
                            ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(varEvalRec.EvaluatorEmailAddress, ""))
                        ) > 0
                        ||
                        CountIf(
                            colUserEmails As ue,
                            ue.Email =
                                Lower(
                                    Coalesce(
                                        varEvalRec.'Created By'.Email,
                                        ""
                                    )
                                )
                        ) > 0
                        ||
                        !IsBlank(
                            LookUp(
                                varEvalRec.Collaborators As collab,
                                CountIf(
                                    colUserEmails As ue,
                                    ue.Email =
                                        Lower(
                                            Coalesce(
                                                collab.Email,
                                                Substitute(
                                                    collab.Claims,
                                                    "i:0#.f|membership|",
                                                    ""
                                                )
                                            )
                                        )
                                ) > 0
                            )
                        )
                    );

                    // ================================
                    // 11. EXPLICIT VIEW AUTHORIZATION
                    // Blocks unauthorized deep links before lock claim or data load.
                    // ================================
                    Set(
                        varCanViewEvaluation,
                        varIsAppAdmin
                        ||
                        varIsHS
                        ||
                        varIsPlannerPM
                        ||
                        varIsCollaborator
                        ||
                        CountIf(
                            colUserEmails As ue,
                            ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(varPlannerHeader.'Prepared By Email', ""))
                        ) > 0
                    );

                    If(
                        !varCanViewEvaluation,

                        Set(
                            varErrorMessage,
                            "This evaluation link is invalid, expired, or you do not have access."
                        );
                        Set(varErrorPopUp, true);
                        UpdateContext({
                            varLoading: false,
                            varShowChecklist: false
                        }),

                        // ELSE: User can view evaluation

                        // ================================
                        // 12. COMPLETED / VIEW-ONLY FLAG
                        // ================================
                        Set(
                            varIsEvaluationCompleted,
                            Coalesce(varEvalRec.Completed, false)
                            ||
                            varEvalRec.EvaluationStatus.Value in [
                                "Accepted",
                                "Rejected",
                                "Not Required",
                                "Complete"
                            ]
                        );

                        // ================================
                        // 13. LOCK CHECK
                        // ================================
                        Set(
                            varIsLocked,
                            !IsBlank(varEvalRec.EditingBy)
                            &&
                            Lower(varEvalRec.EditingBy) <> Lower(User().Email)
                            &&
                            DateDiff(
                                varEvalRec.EditingAt,
                                Now(),
                                TimeUnit.Minutes
                            ) <= Coalesce(varEditLockMinutes, 30)
                        );

                        If(
                            varIsLocked,

                            Set(
                                varErrorMessage,
                                "This evaluation is currently being edited by " &
                                varEvalRec.EditingBy &
                                ". Please try again later."
                            );
                            Set(varErrorPopUp, true);
                            UpdateContext({
                                varLoading: false,
                                varShowChecklist: false
                            }),

                            // ELSE: Not locked

                            // ================================
                            // 14. CLAIM LOCK ONLY IF NOT COMPLETED
                            // ================================
                            If(
                                !varIsEvaluationCompleted,
                                Set(
                                    varEvalRec,
                                    Patch(
                                        'RAMS Evaluation Table',
                                        { ID: varEvalRec.ID },
                                        {
                                            EditingBy: User().Email,
                                            EditingAt: Now()
                                        }
                                    )
                                )
                            );

                            // ================================
                            // 15. PREFILL VALUES / HEADER CONTEXT
                            // ================================
                            Set(__prefill_RAMSTitle, varPlannerRow.Activities);
                            Set(__prefill_RiskRating, varPlannerRow.'Risk Assessed'.Value);
                            Set(__prefill_ReceivedDate, varPlannerRow.'Received Date');
                            Set(__prefill_DocRevision, varPlannerRow.'Doc Revision');

                            Set(varCurrentProjectName, varPlannerHeader.'Project Name'.Value);
                            Set(varCurrentRAMSTitle, varPlannerHeader.Title);
                            Set(varCurrentCreatedBy, varPlannerHeader.'Prepared By');
                            Set(varCurrentHSRep, varPlannerHeader.HSValidators);

                            // ================================
                            // 16. LOAD RAMS HAZARDS ONLY
                            // COSHH loads only on COSHH screen.
                            // ================================
                            If(
                                IsEmpty(colCurrentHazards)
                                ||
                                varLoadedHazardsEvalID <> gblActiveEvaluationID,

                                ClearCollect(
                                    colCurrentHazards,
                                    SortByColumns(
                                        AddColumns(
                                            Filter(
                                                'RAMS Hazards Checklist',
                                                EvaluationID = gblActiveEvaluationID &&
                                                IsCOSHH = false
                                            ),
                                            'IsDirty',
                                            false
                                        ),
                                        "HazardsNo",
                                        SortOrder.Ascending
                                    )
                                );

                                Set(varLoadedHazardsEvalID, gblActiveEvaluationID)
                            );

                            // ================================
                            // 17. SEED RAMS HAZARDS ONLY IF MISSING
                            // This also protects against broken records.
                            // ================================
                            If(
                                IsEmpty(colCurrentHazards),

                                Clear(colUploadBuffer);

                                Collect(
                                    colUploadBuffer,
                                    ForAll(
                                        colRAMSTemplate,
                                        {
                                            Title: "Hazard Q" & num & " - " & gblActiveEvaluationID,
                                            EvaluationID: gblActiveEvaluationID,
                                            PlannerID: gblPlannerID,
                                            PlannerRowID: Value(gblPlannerRowID),
                                            HazardsNo: num,
                                            COSHHNumber: 0,
                                            HazardsIdentified: desc,
                                            IsCOSHH: false,
                                            SuitableControls: {
                                                '@odata.type': "#Microsoft.Azure.Connectors.SharePoint.SPListExpandedReference",
                                                Value: "Not Applicable"
                                            }
                                        }
                                    )
                                );

                                Collect(
                                    'RAMS Hazards Checklist',
                                    colUploadBuffer
                                );

                                Clear(colUploadBuffer);

                                ClearCollect(
                                    colCurrentHazards,
                                    SortByColumns(
                                        AddColumns(
                                            Filter(
                                                'RAMS Hazards Checklist',
                                                EvaluationID = gblActiveEvaluationID &&
                                                IsCOSHH = false
                                            ),
                                            'IsDirty',
                                            false
                                        ),
                                        "HazardsNo",
                                        SortOrder.Ascending
                                    )
                                );

                                Set(varLoadedHazardsEvalID, gblActiveEvaluationID)
                            );

                            // ================================
                            // 18. SELF-HEAL SEEDED FLAG
                            // ================================
                            If(
                                !Coalesce(varEvalRec.HasSeededHazards, false)
                                &&
                                !IsEmpty(colCurrentHazards),

                                Set(
                                    varEvalRec,
                                    Patch(
                                        'RAMS Evaluation Table',
                                        { ID: varEvalRec.ID },
                                        {
                                            HasSeededHazards: true,
                                            EditingBy: If(
                                                varIsEvaluationCompleted,
                                                varEvalRec.EditingBy,
                                                User().Email
                                            ),
                                            EditingAt: If(
                                                varIsEvaluationCompleted,
                                                varEvalRec.EditingAt,
                                                Now()
                                            )
                                        }
                                    )
                                )
                            );

                            // ================================
                            // 19. EDITABILITY
                            // View access and edit access are separate.
                            // ================================
                            Set(
                                varChecklistEditable,
                                !varIsEvaluationCompleted
                                &&
                                (
                                    varIsCollaborator
                                    ||
                                    varIsHS
                                    ||
                                    varIsPlannerPM
                                    ||
                                    varIsAppAdmin
                                )
                            );

                            Set(
                                varApprovalRole,
                                If(
                                    varIsHS,
                                    "HS",
                                    If(
                                        varIsPlannerPM,
                                        "PM",
                                        "Collaborator"
                                    )
                                )
                            );

                            Set(varHasEvaluationAttachment, !IsEmpty(varEvalRec.Attachments));

                            // ================================
                            // 20. DISPLAY HELPERS
                            // ================================
                            Set(varPlannerRowRec, varPlannerRow);

                            Set(
                                varLastSavedName,
                                Trim(
                                    Left(
                                        varEvalRec.'Modified By'.DisplayName,
                                        Find(
                                            "(",
                                            varEvalRec.'Modified By'.DisplayName & "("
                                        ) - 1
                                    )
                                )
                            );

                            // ================================
                            // 21. SUCCESS
                            // ================================
                            Set(varErrorPopUp, false);
                            Set(varErrorMessage, Blank());

                            UpdateContext(
                                {
                                    varProgressCount:
                                        CountIf(
                                            colCurrentHazards,
                                            SuitableControls.Value <> "Not Applicable" &&
                                            !IsBlank(SuitableControls.Value)
                                        ),
                                    varShowChecklist: true,
                                    varLoading: false
                                }
                            );

                            Set(varHeaderRender, true)
                        )
                    )
                )
            )
        );
    Children:
      - ScreenContainer1:
          Control: GroupContainer@1.5.0
          Variant: AutoLayout
          Properties:
            Fill: =RGBA(241, 245, 249, 0.56)
            Height: =Parent.Height
            LayoutAlignItems: =LayoutAlignItems.Stretch
            LayoutDirection: =LayoutDirection.Vertical

                      Control: Button@0.0.45
                      Properties:
                        Align: =Align.Left
                        Appearance: ='ButtonCanvas.Appearance'.Secondary
                        BorderThickness: =0
                        Font: =Font.'Lato Light'
                        FontSize: =If(ScreenSize.Medium, 12,14)
                        FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
                        Icon: ="ArrowLeft"
                        IconStyle: ='ButtonCanvas.IconStyle'.Filled
                        OnSelect: |-
                          =UpdateContext({ varIsSaving: true });

                          // 1. Sync current gallery values into local collection.
                          // This is local only, so it is fast.
                          ForAll(
                              HazardsGallery.AllItems As GalleryRow,
                              Patch(
                                  colCurrentHazards,
                                  LookUp(
                                      colCurrentHazards,
                                      ID = GalleryRow.ID
                                  ),
                                  {
                                      HazardsIdentified: GalleryRow.lblHazardDescription.Value,
                                      SuitableControls: {
                                          '@odata.type': "#Microsoft.Azure.Connectors.SharePoint.SPListExpandedReference",
                                          Value: GalleryRow.HazardSelection.Selected.Value
                                      },
                                      Comments: GalleryRow.Comment.Value
                                  }
                              )
                          );

                          // 2. Save only changed rows to SharePoint.
                          ForAll(
                              Filter(
                                  colCurrentHazards,
                                  IsDirty = true
                              ) As DirtyRow,
                              Patch(
                                  'RAMS Hazards Checklist',
                                  { ID: DirtyRow.ID },
                                  {
                                      HazardsIdentified: DirtyRow.HazardsIdentified,
                                      SuitableControls: DirtyRow.SuitableControls,
                                      Comments: DirtyRow.Comments
                                  }
                              )
                          );

                          // 3. Mark saved rows as clean.
                          UpdateIf(
                              colCurrentHazards,
                              IsDirty = true,
                              { IsDirty: false }
                          );

                          // 2. Release evaluation lock
                          Patch(
                              'RAMS Evaluation Table',
                              { ID: varEvalRec.ID },
                              {
                                  EditingBy: Blank(),
                                  EditingAt: Blank(),
                                  LastSavedByEmail: User().Email,
                                  LastSavedOn: Now()
                              }
                          );

                          UpdateContext({ varIsSaving: false });

                          Navigate(
                              'RAMS Planner',
                              ScreenTransition.Fade,
                              {
                                  paramPlannerID: gblPlannerID,
                                  paramReturnPlannerRowID: gblPlannerRowID
                              }
                          );
                        Text: ="Back"
                        VerticalAlign: =VerticalAlign.Middle
                        X: =40
                        Y: =40

                      Variant: GridLayout

                        LayoutGridRows: =1
                        LayoutMinHeight: =0
                        LayoutMinWidth: =16
                        PaddingBottom: =0
                        PaddingTop: =0
                        Width: =

                      Properties:

                        Font: =Font.'Lato Light'

                  - RefreshButton:
                      Control: Button@0.0.45
                      Properties:
                        Align: =Align.Left
                        Appearance: ='ButtonCanvas.Appearance'.Secondary
                        BorderColor: =RGBA(253, 207, 180, 1)
                        BorderStyle: =BorderStyle.Dotted
                        BorderThickness: =0.5
                        Font: =Font.'Lato Light'
                        FontColor: =RGBA(240, 98, 15, 1)
                        FontSize: =If(ScreenSize.Medium, 12,14)
                        FontWeight: =FontWeight.Bold
                        Icon: ="ArrowSync"
                        IconStyle: ='ButtonCanvas.IconStyle'.Filled
                        OnSelect: |-
                          =Refresh('RAMS Evaluation Table');

                          Set(
                              varEvalRec,
                              LookUp(
                                  'RAMS Evaluation Table',
                                  ID = varResolvedEvalItemID
                              )
                          );

                          Set(
                              varIsLocked,
                              !IsBlank(varEvalRec.EditingBy)
                              &&
                              Lower(varEvalRec.EditingBy) <> Lower(User().Email)
                              &&
                              DateDiff(
                                  varEvalRec.EditingAt,
                                  Now(),
                                  TimeUnit.Minutes
                              ) <= Coalesce(varEditLockMinutes, 30)
                          );

                          If(
                              varIsLocked,

                              Notify(
                                  "Still locked by " & varEvalRec.EditingBy,
                                  NotificationType.Warning,
                                  2000
                              ),

                              // Not locked anymore, so claim lock and show checklist
                              Set(
                                  varEvalRec,
                                  Patch(
                                      'RAMS Evaluation Table',
                                      { ID: varEvalRec.ID },
                                      {
                                          EditingBy: User().Email,
                                          EditingAt: Now()
                                      }
                                  )
                              );

                              Set(varErrorPopUp, false);
                              Set(varErrorMessage, Blank());

                              UpdateContext(
                                  {
                                      varLoading: false,
                                      varShowChecklist: true
                                  }
                              );

                              Notify(
                                  "This evaluation is now ready for you to edit.",
                                  NotificationType.Success,
                                  2000
                              )
                          );
                        Text: ="Check Again"
                        VerticalAlign: =VerticalAlign.Middle
                        Width: =115
                        X: =40
                        Y: =40

      OnVisible: "=// ================================\r\n// RAMS COSHH Assessment Checklist - OnVisible\r\n// Optimised version\r\n// - Uses same evaluation lock as main Evaluation Checklist\r\n// - Loads COSHH only on COSHH screen\r\n// - Seeds COSHH only if COSHH rows do not exist\r\n// ================================\r\n\r\n\r\n// ================================\r\n// 1. START LOADING / RESET VIEW STATE\r\n// ================================\r\nUpdateContext({\r\n    varLoading: true,\r\n    varShowCOSHHChecklist: false\r\n});\r\n\r\n\r\n// ================================\r\n// 2. REFRESH LATEST EVALUATION LOCK / STATUS\r\n// Keep this refresh because COSHH must respect latest edit lock.\r\n// ================================\r\nRefresh('RAMS Evaluation Table');\r\n\r\n\r\n// ================================\r\n// 3. LOAD CURRENT EVALUATION RECORD\r\n// ================================\r\nSet(\r\n    varEvalRec,\r\n    LookUp(\r\n        'RAMS Evaluation Table',\r\n        EvaluationID = gblActiveEvaluationID\r\n    )\r\n);\r\n\r\n\r\n// ================================\r\n// 4. GUARD: EVALUATION RECORD MISSING\r\n// ================================\r\nIf(\r\n    IsBlank(varEvalRec),\r\n\r\n    Notify(\r\n        \"Evaluation record could not be loaded for this COSHH checklist.\",\r\n        NotificationType.Error,\r\n        3000\r\n    );\r\n\r\n    UpdateContext({\r\n        varLoading: false,\r\n        varShowCOSHHChecklist: false\r\n    }),\r\n\r\n    // ELSE: evaluation exists\r\n\r\n    // ================================\r\n    // 5. COMPLETED / VIEW-ONLY FLAG\r\n    // ================================\r\n    Set(\r\n        varIsEvaluationCompleted,\r\n        Coalesce(varEvalRec.Completed, false)\r\n        ||\r\n        varEvalRec.EvaluationStatus.Value in [\r\n            \"Accepted\",\r\n            \"Rejected\",\r\n            \"Not Required\",\r\n            \"Complete\"\r\n        ]\r\n    );\r\n\r\n    // ================================\r\n    // 6. CHECK IF ANOTHER USER OWNS ACTIVE LOCK\r\n    // ================================\r\n    Set(\r\n        varIsLocked,\r\n        !IsBlank(varEvalRec.EditingBy)\r\n        &&\r\n        Lower(varEvalRec.EditingBy) <> Lower(User().Email)\r\n        &&\r\n        DateDiff(\r\n            varEvalRec.EditingAt,\r\n            Now(),\r\n            TimeUnit.Minutes\r\n        ) <= Coalesce(varEditLockMinutes, 30)\r\n    );\r\n\r\n    // ================================\r\n    // 7. IF LOCKED BY ANOTHER USER, BLOCK\r\n    // ================================\r\n    If(\r\n        varIsLocked,\r\n\r\n        Notify(\r\n            \"This evaluation is currently being edited by \" &\r\n            varEvalRec.EditingBy &\r\n            \". Please try again later.\",\r\n            NotificationType.Warning,\r\n            3000\r\n        );\r\n\r\n        UpdateContext({\r\n            varLoading: false,\r\n            varShowCOSHHChecklist: false\r\n        });\r\n\r\n        Back(),\r\n\r\n        // ELSE: not locked\r\n\r\n        // ================================\r\n        // 8. CLAIM / REFRESH LOCK ONLY IF NOT COMPLETED\r\n        // ================================\r\n        If(\r\n            !varIsEvaluationCompleted,\r\n            Set(\r\n                varEvalRec,\r\n                Patch(\r\n                    'RAMS Evaluation Table',\r\n                    { ID: varEvalRec.ID },\r\n                    {\r\n                        EditingBy: User().Email,\r\n                        EditingAt: Now()\r\n                    }\r\n                )\r\n            )\r\n        );\r\n\r\n        // ================================\r\n        // 9. LOAD COSHH ITEMS ONLY IF NEEDED\r\n        // ================================\r\n        If(\r\n            IsEmpty(colCOSHHItems)\r\n            ||\r\n            varLoadedCOSHHEvalID <> gblActiveEvaluationID,\r\n\r\n            ClearCollect(\r\n                colCOSHHItems,\r\n                SortByColumns(\r\n                    AddColumns(\r\n                        Filter(\r\n                            'RAMS Hazards Checklist',\r\n                            EvaluationID = gblActiveEvaluationID\r\n                            &&\r\n                            IsCOSHH = true\r\n                        ),\r\n                        'IsDirty',\r\n                        false\r\n                    ),\r\n                    \"COSHHNumber\",\r\n                    SortOrder.Ascending\r\n                )\r\n            );\r\n\r\n            Set(varLoadedCOSHHEvalID, gblActiveEvaluationID)\r\n        );\r\n\r\n        // ================================\r\n        // 10. SEED COSHH ONLY IF EMPTY\r\n        // This happens only when the COSHH screen is opened.\r\n        // ================================\r\n        If(\r\n            IsEmpty(colCOSHHItems),\r\n\r\n            Clear(colUploadBuffer);\r\n\r\n            Collect(\r\n                colUploadBuffer,\r\n                ForAll(\r\n                    colCOSHHTemplate,\r\n                    {\r\n                        Title: \"COSHH Q\" & num & \" - \" & gblActiveEvaluationID,\r\n                        EvaluationID: gblActiveEvaluationID,\r\n                        PlannerID: gblPlannerID,\r\n                        PlannerRowID: Value(gblPlannerRowID),\r\n                        HazardsNo: 0,\r\n                        COSHHNumber: num,\r\n                        HazardsIdentified: desc,\r\n                        IsCOSHH: true,\r\n                        SuitableControls: {\r\n                            '@odata.type': \"#Microsoft.Azure.Connectors.SharePoint.SPListExpandedReference\",\r\n                            Value: \"Not Applicable\"\r\n                        }\r\n                    }\r\n                )\r\n            );\r\n\r\n            Collect(\r\n                'RAMS Hazards Checklist',\r\n                colUploadBuffer\r\n            );\r\n\r\n            Clear(colUploadBuffer);\r\n\r\n            ClearCollect(\r\n                colCOSHHItems,\r\n                SortByColumns(\r\n                    AddColumns(\r\n                        Filter(\r\n                            'RAMS Hazards Checklist',\r\n                            EvaluationID = gblActiveEvaluationID\r\n                            &&\r\n                            IsCOSHH = true\r\n                        ),\r\n                        'IsDirty',\r\n                        false\r\n                    ),\r\n                    \"COSHHNumber\",\r\n                    SortOrder.Ascending\r\n                )\r\n            );\r\n\r\n            Set(varLoadedCOSHHEvalID, gblActiveEvaluationID)\r\n        );\r\n\r\n        // ================================\r\n        // 11. WORK OUT COLLABORATOR / ACCESS\r\n        // ================================\r\n        Set(\r\n            varIsCollaborator,\r\n            varIsAppAdmin\r\n            ||\r\n            CountIf(\r\n                colUserEmails As ue,\r\n                ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(varEvalRec.LastSavedByEmail, \"\"))\r\n            ) > 0\r\n            ||\r\n            CountIf(\r\n                colUserEmails As ue,\r\n                ue.Email = Lower(Coalesce(varEvalRec.EvaluatorEmailAddress, \"\"))\r\n            ) > 0\r\n            ||\r\n            CountIf(\r\n                colUserEmails As ue,\r\n                ue.Email =\r\n                    Lower(\r\n                        Coalesce(\r\n                            varEvalRec.'Created By'.Email,\r\n                            \"\"\r\n                        )\r\n                    )\r\n            ) > 0\r\n            ||\r\n            !IsBlank(\r\n                LookUp(\r\n                    varEvalRec.Collaborators As collab,\r\n                    CountIf(\r\n                        colUserEmails As ue,\r\n                        ue.Email =\r\n                            Lower(\r\n                                Coalesce(\r\n                                    collab.Email,\r\n                                    Substitute(\r\n                                        collab.Claims,\r\n                                        \"i:0#.f|membership|\",\r\n                                        \"\"\r\n                                    )\r\n                                )\r\n                            )\r\n                    ) > 0\r\n                )\r\n            )\r\n        );\r\n\r\n        // ================================\r\n        // 12. COSHH EDITABLE FLAG\r\n        // ================================\r\n        Set(\r\n            varChecklistEditable,\r\n            !varIsEvaluationCompleted\r\n            &&\r\n            !varIsLocked\r\n            &&\r\n            (\r\n                varIsCollaborator\r\n                ||\r\n                varIsHS\r\n                ||\r\n                varIsAppAdmin\r\n                \r\n||\r\nvarIsPlannerPM\r\n\r\n            )\r\n        );\r\n\r\n        // ================================\r\n        // 13.                                   Filter(

                                      &&

                                  )

                                  NotificationType.Warning,

                          // 1. Sync current COSHH gallery values into local collection.
                          ForAll(
                              HazardsGallery_1.AllItems As GalleryRow,
                              Patch(
                                  colCOSHHItems,

                                      colCOSHHItems,

                                  ),

                                  }
                              )
                          );

                          // 2. Save only changed COSHH rows.
                          ForAll(
                              Filter(
                                  colCOSHHItems,
                                  IsDirty = true
                              ) As DirtyRow,
                              Patch(
                                  'RAMS Hazards Checklist',
                                  { ID: DirtyRow.ID },
                                  {
                                      HazardsIdentified: DirtyRow.HazardsIdentified,
                                      SuitableControls: DirtyRow.SuitableControls,
                                      Comments: DirtyRow.Comments
                                  }
                              )
                          );

                          // 3. Mark saved COSHH rows as clean.
                          UpdateIf(
                              colCOSHHItems,
                              IsDirty = true,
                              { IsDirty: false }
                          );


                          // 2. Keep / refresh lock
                          // Do NOT clear EditingBy / EditingAt because user is still inside same evaluation
                          Set(
                              varEvalRec,
                              Patch(
                                  'RAMS Evaluation Table',
                                  { ID: varEvalRec.ID },
                                  {
                                      EditingBy: User().Email,
                                      EditingAt: Now(),
                                      LastSavedByEmail: User().Email,
                                      LastSavedOn: Now()
                                  }
                              )
                          );

                          UpdateContext({ varIsSaving: false });

                          // 3. Go back to main Evaluation Checklist
                          Navigate(
                              'RAMS Evaluation Checklist',
                              ScreenTransition.CoverRight
                          ));

                  - AddnewHazardCoshh:
                      Control: Button@0.0.45
                      Properties:
                        Appearance: ='ButtonCanvas.Appearance'.Secondary
                        Font: =Font.'Lato Light'
                        FontColor: =RGBA(15, 108, 189, 1)
                        FontSize: =If(ScreenSize.Medium, 12,14)
                        FontWeight: =FontWeight.Bold
                        Icon: ="Add"
                        OnSelect: |-
                          =UpdateContext({ varLoading: true });

                          // 1. SYNC UI TO LOCAL COLLECTION (Instant UI capture)
                          ForAll(HazardsGallery_1.AllItems As _row,
                              Patch(colCOSHHItems, LookUp(colCOSHHItems, ID = _row.ID), {
                                  HazardsIdentified: _row.lblHazardDescription_1.Value,
                                  SuitableControls: { Value: _row.HazardSelection_1.Selected.Value },
                                  Comments: _row.Comment_1.Value
                              })
                          );

                          // 2. BULK SAVE TO SHAREPOINT (Prevents data loss during refresh)
                          Patch('RAMS Hazards Checklist', 
                              ShowColumns(colCOSHHItems, 'ID', 'HazardsIdentified', 'SuitableControls', 'Comments')
                          );

                          // 3. CREATE THE NEW BLANK ROW
                          UpdateContext({ varNextCOSHHNum: CountRows(colCOSHHItems) + 1 });
                          Patch('RAMS Hazards Checklist', Defaults('RAMS Hazards Checklist'), {
                              Title: "Custom COSHH " & varNextCOSHHNum & " - " & gblActiveEvaluationID,
                              EvaluationID: gblActiveEvaluationID,
                              PlannerID: gblPlannerID,
                              PlannerRowID: Value(gblPlannerRowID),
                              COSHHNumber: varNextCOSHHNum,
                              IsCOSHH: true,
                              HazardsIdentified: "[New COSHH Substance/Risk]",
                              SuitableControls: { '@odata.type': "#Microsoft.Azure.Connectors.SharePoint.SPListExpandedReference", Value: "Not Applicable" }
                          });

                          // 4. REFRESH COLLECTION (The Single Network Pass)
                          ClearCollect(colCOSHHItems, 
                              Sort(Filter('RAMS Hazards Checklist', EvaluationID = gblActiveEvaluationID && IsCOSHH = true), COSHHNumber, SortOrder.Ascending)
                          );
                          Set(
                              varEvalRec,
                              Patch(
                                  'RAMS Evaluation Table',
                                  { ID: varEvalRec.ID },
                                  {
                                      EditingBy: User().Email,
                                      EditingAt: Now(),
                                      LastSavedByEmail: User().Email,
                                      LastSavedOn: Now()
                                  }
                              )
                          );
                          UpdateContext({ varLoading: false });
                          Notify("Progress saved and new row added.", NotificationType.Success, 500);
                        Text: ="Add New Hazard"
                        X: =40
                        Y: =40

                        Y: =55
                  - SaveChecklist_1:
                      Control: Button@0.0.45
                      Properties:
                        Appearance: ='ButtonCanvas.Appearance'.Secondary
                        DisplayMode: |+
                          =
                              DisplayMode.Edit
                        Font: =Font.'Lato Light'
                        FontColor: =RGBA(9, 33, 98, 1)
                        FontSize: =If(ScreenSize.Medium, 12,14)
                        FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
                        Icon: ="Save"
                        IconStyle: ='ButtonCanvas.IconStyle'.Outline
                        Layout: ='ButtonCanvas.Layout'.IconAfter
                        OnSelect: |+
                          =If(
                              CountRows(
                                  Filter(
                                      HazardsGallery_1.AllItems,
                                      HazardSelection_1.Selected.Value = "No"
                                      &&
                                      IsBlank(Trim(Comment_1.Value))
                                  )
                              ) > 0,

                              Notify(
                                  "Please add comments for all COSHH checklist rows where control measure is selected as No.",
                                  NotificationType.Warning,
                                  3000
                              ),

                             // 1. Sync current COSHH gallery values into local collection.
                          ForAll(
                              HazardsGallery_1.AllItems As GalleryRow,
                              Patch(
                                  colCOSHHItems,
                                  LookUp(
                                      colCOSHHItems,
                                      ID = GalleryRow.ID
                                  ),
                                  {
                                      HazardsIdentified: GalleryRow.lblHazardDescription_1.Value,
                                      SuitableControls: {
                                          '@odata.type': "#Microsoft.Azure.Connectors.SharePoint.SPListExpandedReference",
                                          Value: GalleryRow.HazardSelection_1.Selected.Value
                                      },
                                      Comments: GalleryRow.Comment_1.Value
                                  }
                              )
                          );

                          // 2. Save only changed COSHH rows.
                          ForAll(
                              Filter(
                                  colCOSHHItems,
                                  IsDirty = true
                              ) As DirtyRow,
                              Patch(
                                  'RAMS Hazards Checklist',
                                  { ID: DirtyRow.ID },
                                  {
                                      HazardsIdentified: DirtyRow.HazardsIdentified,
                                      SuitableControls: DirtyRow.SuitableControls,
                                      Comments: DirtyRow.Comments
                                  }
                              )
                          );

                          // 3. Mark saved COSHH rows as clean.
                          UpdateIf(
                              colCOSHHItems,
                              IsDirty = true,
                              { IsDirty: false }
                          ));


                              UpdateContext({ varIsSaving: false });

                              Notify(
                                  "COSHH Progress Saved Successfully",
                                  NotificationType.Success,
                                  1000
                              )
                        Text: ="Save"
                        Width: =135
                        X: =40
                        Y: =111
